## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소의 깨진 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 직접 살펴보고 처리할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 🙂
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봬요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요!
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、**TCPは届いたことを確認して順番どおりに届ける「信頼性重視」、UDPは確認も順序保証もせず速さを優先する「軽量・即時重視」**という点です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local, que aún no has compartido, antes de integrarla: pones tus commits encima de la última versión de la base y el historial queda lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya esté compartida o quieras conservar tal cual cómo ocurrió el trabajo, porque reescribir historial que otros ya tienen causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的、面向连接的字节流，会确认、重传、排序；UDP 只管尽快发包，不保证送达、不保证顺序，但没有连接和确认的开销，所以快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpublished branch to update it onto the latest main and keep history linear before you open a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared with others, or when you want the record of when the work actually happened.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，我按顺序做：先换输入框，再接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，每件做完告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本任务只做第一件：把登录表单换成新的输入框组件。后面两件（接短信验证码、端到端测试）由后续任务做，这次不要碰。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单里的输入框都换成新的输入框组件，表单的行为和原来一致（输入、校验提示、提交）。 ⏎ - 现有测试全部通过，报告里附上测试命令和输出结果。 ⏎ - 在仓库里提交到本任务自己的分支。 ⏎  ⏎ 不要做： ⏎ - 不做短信验证码，不补端到端测试。 ⏎ - 不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/ducter 仓库。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做

```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径传给 `bun test` 就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

它也接受路径片段，比如 `bun test foo` 会跑所有文件名含 `foo` 的测试。如果想只跑某个用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data, so a crash mid-write can be recovered by replaying the log. It trades one extra sequential write per change for durability and fast recovery without rewriting the whole data file each time.
```

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?
  [partner message] The Mac mini is the better home for me. It has the same chip and memory for $400 less, and the Air's portability and battery would go unused on a desk.

| | Mac mini M4 | MacBook Air M4 |
|---|---|---|
| Price | $599 | $999 |
| Memory | 16 GB | 16 GB |
| Power | Always plugged in | Runs on battery |
| Cooling | Fan, holds speed under long jobs | No fan, can slow down during long heavy work |
| Ethernet | Built in | Needs an adapter |
| Moves with you | No | Yes |

The Air only makes sense if you want one machine that you carry around and that runs me when you're out. For a box that stays home, I'd pick the mini.
```

要做的：在同一分支（ducter/compaction-scheduler）上，按 review 里关于锁粒度的意见把锁改细，让 compaction 调度不再长时间持有大锁。
完成标准：测试全部通过，并报告跑了哪些测试、结果如何；说明每处锁的改动。
不做的事：不合并、不推送到基线分支、不开新 PR；草稿 PR 保持草稿。只在分支上提交。
  [also used tools] task_find
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="low">还没看到</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">每天的票价</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
function render(d){
  var rows = (d && d.trains) || [];
  var under = rows.filter(function(t){ return t.price < 600; });
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var low = document.getElementById('low');
  if (!rows.length) { cnt.textContent = '—'; low.textContent = '还没看到'; }
  else {
    cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    low.textContent = under.length ? '最低 ¥' + Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function(t){return t.price;})) : '暂时没有 600 以下的';
  }
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  rows.forEach(function(t){
    var r = document.createElement('div');
    r.className = 'k-row';
    r.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + (t.price < 600 ? '' : '（超过 600）') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + (t.left ? '二等座余 ' + t.left + ' 张' : '') + ' · ' + (t.day || '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + t.price + '</div>';
    list.appendChild(r);
  });
  if (!rows.length) { list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">明早开始记，每天的票价会排在这里。</div>'; }
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(render); });
</script>

      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms' + (n.stalls != null ? ' · ' + n.stalls + ' compaction stalls' : '');
      main.appendChild(name);
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = fmtWrites(n.writes);
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    ducter.summary('Night ' + top.night + ': ' + fmtWrites(top.writes) + ' writes/s');
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0},{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4}]}

<script>
function yuan(n){return "¥" + Number(n||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.yuan || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = yuan(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = yuan(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + yuan(total) : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记。点下面的「记一笔」。</div>
  </div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>时间</label><input class="k-input" id="ti" placeholder="如 14:20"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="如 100"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const today = () => { const d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); };
let feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];

function save() { ducter.set('feeds', feeds); }

function render() {
  const t = today();
  const idx = feeds.map((f, i) => ({ f, i })).filter(x => x.f.d === t)
                    .sort((a, b) => a.f.h < b.f.h ? -1 : a.f.h > b.f.h ? 1 : 0); … mentById('list').innerHTML = idx.map(x =>
    '<div class="k-row" data-i="' + x.i + '"><div class="k-row-main">' + x.f.h +
    '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + x.f.ml + ' ml</div></div>').join('');
  document.getElementById('empty').hidden = idx.length > 0;
  ducter.summary(idx.length ? '今天 ' + idx.length + ' 次,合计 ' + sum + 'ml' : '今天还没记');
}

document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const i = Number(row.dataset.i);
  const f = feeds[i];
  ducter.ask('删掉 ' + f.h + ' 的 ' + f.ml + 'ml 这一次?').then(() => {
    feeds.splice(i, 1); save(); render();
  });
});

document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const h = document.getElementById('ti').value.trim();
  const ml = Number(document.getElementById('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(h) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  feeds.push({ d: today(), h: h.padStart(5, '0'), ml });
  save();
  document.getElementById('ti').value = '';
  document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
  document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
  render();
});

ducter.onChange(() => { feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || []; render(); });
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-21","h":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-21","h":"09:05","ml":90}]}

  [partner message] Done. It's a little page called "PRs waiting" with your four PRs; tick each one off as it lands and the count drops. Its card is below.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1851 characters): <style>
.k-row.done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-tick{width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;border:1.5px solid var(--d-line);background:none;color:var(--d-card);font-size:14px;flex:none;cursor:pointer}
.k-tick[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--d-good);border-color:var(--d-good)}
</style>
<main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="n">–</div><div class="k-sum-sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
var SEED=ducter.get("seed") || [];
var items=SEED;
function save(){ducter.set('prs',items);}
function render(){
  var list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  items.forEach(function(it){
    var row=document.createElement('div');
    row.className='k-row'+(it.done?' done':'');
    var main=document.createElement('div');
    main.className='k-row-main';
    var t=document.createElement('div');
    t.textContent=it.pr+' · '+it.what;
    var s=document.createElement('div');
    s.className='k-row-sub';
    s.textContent=it.why;
    main.appendChild(t);main.appendChild(s);
    var b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='k-tick';
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',it.done?'true':'false');
    b.setAttribute('aria-label','Landed');
    b.textContent=it.done?'✓':'';
    b.onclick=function(){it.done=!it.done;save();render();};
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(b);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var left=items.filter(function(x){return !x.done;}).length;
  document.getElementById('n').textContent=left;
  ducter.summary(left+' waiting');
}
function load(){
  var s=ducter.get('prs');
  if(Array.isArray(s)) items=s;
  render();
}
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","pr":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","why":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"m10287","pr":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","why":"CI is failing","done":false},{"id":"h1592","pr":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","why":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"d418","pr":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","why":"Still a draft","done":false}],"seed":[{"id":"d412","pr":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","why":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"m10287","pr":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","why":"CI is failing","done":false},{"id":"h1592","pr":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","why":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"d418","pr":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","why":"Still a draft","done":false}]}

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 风格笔记本电脑图标。屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色，键盘和触控板的细节也都画出来了，整体柔和温暖。

很适合放在空状态里。空白的屏幕本身就是"这里还没有内容"的意思，一眼就能懂。配色柔和，不会让人觉得出了错，反而有点亲切。

用的时候注意两点：一是它的深色边框是画面里最抢眼的颜色，如果你的产品主色不同，可能需要跟它协调一下；二是它的风格偏可爱，如果界面比较严肃，放大了可能显得幼稚，建议控制在 120 到 160 像素左右，放在标题和说明文字的上方。
```

Do not change the global default for other clusters unless the test cluster setup is the only place the default lives, and say so in the report if that is the case. ⏎  ⏎ Done when: ⏎ - You have found where the WAL flush interval is configured for the test cluster (config file, env var, or test fixture) and changed the value from 200 ms to 50 ms. ⏎ - You report the file(s) changed and the diff. ⏎ - If a quick test or config validation exists for this, run it and report the result. If none exists, say that you did not run one. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not push, open a PR, or merge into the base branch. Commit the change on your own task branch only. Do not change the checkpoint interval (5 minutes) or any other setting. If you cannot find where the test cluster sets this, stop and report what you looked at instead of guessing.
